- **architect**: Design consultant invoked in fresh context for a single architecture question in a devkit project. - **architect**: Use to set implementation direction for an architecture-affecting change — turn an approved PRD or a design brief into a governed ADR or RFC carrying contracts, state machines, and input/output seams. It stops at "ready for review" — never flips a status, never self-assigns an owner. For requirements dispatch analyst; for the mechanical write-up dispatch drafter. Read-only. Use to assess an architecture, review a design or plan for structural soundness, or propose a restructuring. Covers structure only; failure modes of a plan go to `pre-mortem` and security to `security-reviewer`. ([Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/ports/copilot/agent-skills/com.github.copilot/agents/architect.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **architect**: Own the design of a development objective end to end. Establish the spec with the user, discover capability and repository constraints, iterate the design as work completes, and issue approved slices to the orchestrator. Use as the entry point for work that needs a durable design before implementation. Run as the interactive primary agent. ([milkyware/awesome-ai](https://github.com/milkyware/awesome-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-workflow/.apm/agents/architect.agent.md))

([millerlai/claude-all-in-one](https://github.com/millerlai/claude-all-in-one/blob/HEAD/plugins/cai/agents/architect.md))

([mindfold-ai/Trellis](https://github.com/mindfold-ai/Trellis/blob/HEAD/.trellis/agents/architect.md) · 5 copies)

- **architect**: Você é o Architect (Arquiteto de Sistemas/Soluções). Seu foco primário é estrutura, escalabilidade, organização lógica e padrões de design. Você não se preocupa com o custo ou detalhe microscópico de implementação, mas sim se a fundação do tópico proposto é sólida, elegante e se não acumulará dívida técnica (ou dívida de design) estrutural no longo prazo. - **architect**: Build-fleet pattern selector. Reads a PDD with low ambiguity (status `ready-for-architect`) and writes an Architecture Decision Record specifying which UiPath pattern to use — Sequence, REFramework Performer, REFramework Dispatcher, Coded Workflow (C#), Coded Agent (LangGraph / OpenAI Agents / LlamaIndex), Maestro, Action Center, API Workflow, or Document Understanding. Use this agent after Analyst (or Interviewer) sets a candidate to `ready-for-architect`. Architect is the only agent that picks patterns; Conductor never invokes a Forger directly. ([mlbrilliance/uipath-for-coding-agents](https://github.com/mlbrilliance/uipath-for-coding-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/architect.md))
